# R2TR — static reachability tracer receipt

Files: `trace_r2_reachability.py` sha256 08e5145cf95fef3e5e87f56c8e135666a8755a62432785a8b44f5c17e3ae8c73; `test_trace_r2_reachability.py` sha256 12edf2e0b23b2a835b95076b347a0fc8513ff964d61ed65935b91fbffc168880.
`r2tr_report.md` has its final sha256 in `r2tr_sha256.txt`; its own digest cannot be embedded without changing the hashed bytes.
Design: stdlib AST interpretation, import aliases and transitive starter/loader fixed points; recursive cycles, dynamic callbacks, mutation and opaque callees remain explicit UNRESOLVED obligations. Target code is never imported or executed.
D16f/D15: unknown/data-selected branches remain reachable; only literal evaluation or a structurally verified pre-spawn guard excludes them. File and stdin reads reset values to loaded data. Counts and operands are recorded per site/path, with enforcing/selection lines and record/field provenance.
Shell: lex command segments, pipelines and nested substitutions; branch feasibility and loop extent are conservative. Unparsed controls/functions/variable commands are UNRESOLVED. Distinct origins and record selections are matched independently; unrelated refusals never cover missing families.
Limits: finite path witnesses plus explicit cycle/boundary obligations, not a proof about opaque library internals. GAP means the conservative reachable family lacks matching declaration/refusal; shell uncertainty and unknown argument shapes can require source review. No P8-driven tuning occurred.
Test-first: initial 14 tests failed because TR was absent. Before implementation completion, the P7 run exited 1 with F1=1 and F2=1; full failing outputs/chains are preserved in `r2tr_test_first_red.md` and `r2tr_p7_incomplete.json`. Later behavioral RED logs are `r2tr_{hardening,edge,comprehension,sources,dynamic_receiver}_red.txt`.
Frozen receipt: `r2tr_frozen_final.json`; final P7/P8 runs verified tracer/tests unchanged and every traced source hash unchanged after each run. No cross-profile source drift was found.
